represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n//        const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i <        Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char**      } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n <cstring>\\n#include <limits>\\n#include <vector>\\n\\n#include \\\"../common/results_output.hpp\\\"\\n\\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\\n\\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\\nstruct Point {\\n    double x, y;\\n};\\n\\n// Structure to represent a cluster\\nstruct Cluster {\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    int seed_point;\\n};\\n\\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\\n    \\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\\n    int count = 0;\\n    \\n    while (count < N) {\\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\\n        \\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n            group_cnt = N - count;\\n        }\\n        \\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\\n            const double r = frand() * R;\\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\\n            \\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\\n                continue;\\n            }\\n            \\n            points[count] = {x, y};\\n            count++;\\n            group_cnt--;\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\\n}\\n\\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                     const double threshold,\\n                     const int point_count) {\\n    int closest_point = -1;\\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\\n    \\n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\\n        \\n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\\n            closest_point = candidate;\\n        }\\n    }\\n    \\n    return closest_point;\\n}\\n\\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                              const double threshold,\\n                              const int point_count,\\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    \\n    // Add seed point\\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\\n    \\n    // Iteratively add closest points\\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \\n                                             threshold, point_count);\\n        \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if algorithm\\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n                                  const double threshold) {\\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\\n    \\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Main clustering loop\\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\\n        int best_seed = -1;\\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\\n        \\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\\n            \\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \\n                                                       threshold, N, \\n                                                       &candidate_members);\\n            \\n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\\n                best_seed = seed;\\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\\n            }\\n        }\\n        \\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\\n            Cluster cluster;\\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\\n            \\n            // Mark all members as clustered\\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\\n            }\\n            \\n            // 20.0;\n\nstatic const int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\nstatic const int MAX_GRID_BLOCKS = 512;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (host)\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (device)\n__device__ inline double pointDist(const double2 a, const double2 b) {\n    const double dx = a.x - b.x;\n    const double dy = a.y - b.y;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Precompute the full pairwise distance matrix\n__global__ void distMatrixKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                                 double* __restrict__ distmat,\n                                 const int N) {\n    const int j = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = blockIdx.y;\n    if (j < N) {\n        distmat[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = pointDist(points[i], points[j]);\n    }\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster for each seed (one thread block per seed,\n// grid-stride over seeds) and record its cardinality. rebuild launch), the members are\n// recorded in selection order.\n__global__ void growClustersKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                                   const double* __restrict__ distmat,\n                                   const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                   const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                   const int num_seeds,\n                                   const int N,\n                                   const double threshold,\n                                   double* __restrict__ workspace,\n                                   int* __restrict__ cardinalities,\n                                   int* __restrict__ members_out) {\n    __shared__ double sval[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int sidx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int chosen;\n\n    double* md = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n\n    for (int s = blockIdx.x; s < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[s];\n        const double2 seed_pt = points[seed];\n        const double* seed_row =\n            distmat ? distmat + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * N : nullptr;\n\n        for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < N; j += blockDim.x) {\n            if (j == seed) {\n                md[j] = -1.0;\n            } else if (clustered[j]) {\n                md[j] = DBL_MAX;\n            } else {\n                md[j] = seed_row ? seed_row[j] : pointDist(seed_pt, points[j]);\n            }\n        }\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0 && members_out) {\n            members_out[0] = seed;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        int count = 1;\n        while (count < N) {\n            // Block-wide argmin over eligible candidates\n            double best_val = DBL_MAX;\n            int best_idx = -1;\n            for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < N; j += blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = md[j];\n                if (v >= 0.0 && v < threshold && v < best_val) {\n                    best_val = v;\n                    best_idx = j;\n                }\n            }\n            sval[threadIdx.x] = best_val;\n            sidx[threadIdx.x] = best_idx;\n            __syncthreads();\n            for (int off = blockDim.x / 2; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n                if (threadIdx.x < off) {\n                    const double ov = sval[threadIdx.x + off];\n                    const int oi = sidx[threadIdx.x + off];\n                    if (oi >= 0 &&\n                        (sidx[threadIdx.x] < 0 || ov < sval[threadIdx.x] ||\n                         (ov == sval[threadIdx.x] && oi < sidx[threadIdx.x]))) {\n                        sval[threadIdx.x] = ov;\n                        sidx[threadIdx.x] = oi;\n                    }\n                }\n                __syncthreads();\n            }\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n                chosen = sidx[0];\n                if (chosen >= 0) {\n                    md[chosen] = -1.0;\n                    if (members_out) {\n                        members_out[count] = chosen;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n            const int c = chosen;\n            if (c < 0) break;  // No more points can be added\n            count++;\n\n            // Fold the new member's distances into md\n            const double2 c_pt = points[c];\n            const double* c_row =\n                distmat ? distmat + static_cast<size_t>(c) * N : nullptr;\n            for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < N; j += blockDim.x) {\n                const double {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    // Device buffers\n    double2* d_points = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_card, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_workspace,\n                          sizeof(double) * static_cast<size_t>(grid_max) * N));\n\n    static_assert(sizeof(Point) == sizeof(double2), \"Point/double2 layout mismatch\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), sizeof(double2) * N,\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // Precompute the pairwise distance matrix when it fits comfortably\n    {\n        size_t free_mem = 0, total_mem = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_mem, &total_mem));\n        const size_t distmat_bytes = sizeof(double) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N;\n        if (distmat_bytes < static_cast<size_t>(0.8 * free_mem)) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_distmat, distmat_bytes));\n            const dim3 dm_grid((N + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE, N);\n            distMatrixKernel<<<dm_grid, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_points, d_distmat, N);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cardinalities(N);\n    std::vector<int> best_members_host(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int num_seeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, clustered.data(),\n                              sizeof(unsigned char) * N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds, unclustered_indices.data(),\n                              sizeof(int) * num_seeds, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n        // Phase 1: cardinality of the candidate cluster for every seed\n        const int grid = std::min(num_seeds, grid_max);\n        growClustersKernel<<<grid, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n            d_points, d_distmat, d_clustered, d_seeds, num_seeds, N, point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//  - Every unclustered point is tried as a seed; each candidate cluster is\n//    grown by one CUDA thread block (grid-stride over seeds).\n//  - Each block keeps, for every point, the maximum distance to the current\n//    cluster members (\"min-diameter if added\"), updated incrementally after\n//    each addition, and selects the next member with a block-wide argmin\n//    reduction that breaks ties toward the lowest point index, exactly\n//    matching the sequential candidate scan MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign =          const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (host)\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (device)\n__device__ inline double pointDist(const double2 a, const double2 b) {\n    const double dx = a.x - b.x;\n    const double dy = a.y - b.y;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Precompute the full pairwise distance matrix\n__global__ void distMatrixKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                                 double* __restrict__ distmat,\n                                 const int N) {\n    const int j = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = blockIdx.y;\n    if (j < N) {\n        distmat[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = pointDist(points[i], points[j]);\n    }\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster for each seed (one thread block per seed,\n// grid-stride over seeds) and record its cardinality. For every point j,\n// md[j] holds the maximum distance from j to the current cluster members,\n// i.e. the cluster diameter if j were added; md[j] = -1 marks members of\n// this candidate cluster and md[j] = DBL_MAX marks globally clustered\n// points (both are never selected). The next member is the eligible point\n// (md < threshold) with the smallest md, ties broken toward the lowest\n// index to match the sequential scan.\n// If members_out is non-null (single-seed rebuild launch), the members are\n// recorded in selection order.\n__global__ void growClustersKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                                   const double* __restrict__ distmat,\n                                   const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                   const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                   const int num_seeds,\n                                   const int N,\n                                   const double threshold,\n                                   double* __restrict__ workspace,\n                                   int* __restrict__ cardinalities,\n                                   int* __restrict__ members_out) {\n    __shared__ double sval[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int sidx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int chosen;\n\n    double* md = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n\n    for (int s = blockIdx.x; s < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[s];\n        const double2 seed_pt = points[seed];\n        const double* seed_row =\n            distmat ? distmat + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * N : nullptr;\n\n        for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < N; j += blockDim.x) {\n            if (j == seed) {\n                md[j] = -1.0;\n            } else if (clustered[j]) {\n                md[j] = DBL_MAX;\n            } else {\n                md[j] = seed_row ? seed_row[j] : pointDist(seed_pt, points[j]);\n            }\n        }\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0 && members_out) {\n            members_out[0] = seed;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        int count = 1;\n        while (count < N) {\n            // Block-wide argmin over eligible candidates\n            double best_val = DBL_MAX;\n            int best_idx = -1;\n            for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < N; j += blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = md[j];\n                if (v >= 0.0 && v < threshold && v < best_val) {\n                    best_val = v;\n                    best_idx = j;\n                }\n            }\n            sval[threadIdx.x] = best_val;\n            sidx[threadIdx.x] = best_idx;\n            __syncthreads();\n            for (int off = blockDim.x / 2; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n                if (threadIdx.x < off) {\n                    const double ov = sval[threadIdx.x + off];\n                    const int oi = sidx[threadIdx.x + off];\n                    if (oi >= 0 &&\n                        (sidx[threadIdx.x] < 0 || ov < sval[threadIdx.x] ||\n                         (ov == sval[threadIdx.x] && oi < sidx[threadIdx.x]))) {\n                        sval[threadIdx.x] = ov;\n                        sidx[threadIdx.x] = oi;\n                    }\n                }\n                __syncthreads();\n            }\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n                chosen = sidx[0];\n                if (chosen >= 0) {\n                    md[chosen] = -1.0;\n                    if (members_out) {\n                        members_out[count] = chosen;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n            const int c = chosen;\n            if (c < 0) break;  // No more points can be added\n            count++;\n\n            // Fold the new member's distances into md\n            const double2 c_pt = points[c];\n            const double* c_row =\n                distmat ? distmat + static_cast<size_t>(c) * N : nullptr;\n            for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < N; j += blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = md[j];\n                if (v >= 0.0 && v < DBL_MAX) {\n                    const double d = c_row ? c_row[j] : pointDist(c_pt, points[j]);\n                    if (d > v) {\n                        md[j] = d;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n            cardinalities[s] = count;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (CUDA)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    // Device buffers\n    double2* d_points = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_seeds = nullptr;\n    int* d_card = nullptr;\n    int* d_members = nullptr;\n    double* d_workspace = nullptr;\n    double* d_distmat = nullptr;\n\n    const int grid_max = std::min(N, MAX_GRID_BLOCKS);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, sizeof(double2) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, sizeof(unsigned char) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_card, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_workspace,\n                          sizeof(double) * static_cast<size_t>(grid_max) * N));\n\n    static_assert(sizeof(Point) == sizeof(double2), \"Point/double2 layout mismatch\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), sizeof(double2) * N,\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // Precompute the pairwise distance matrix when it fits comfortably\n    {\n        size_t free_mem = 0, total_mem = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_mem, &total_mem));\n        const size_t distmat_bytes = sizeof(double) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N;\n        if (distmat_bytes < static_cast<size_t>(0.8 * free_mem)) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_distmat, distmat_bytes));\n            const dim3 dm_grid((N + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE, N);\n            distMatrixKernel<<<dm_grid, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_points, d_distmat, N);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cardinalities(N);\n    std::vector<int> best_members_host(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int num_seeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, clustered.data(),\n                              sizeof(unsigned char) * N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds, unclustered_indices.data(),\n                              sizeof(int) * num_seeds, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n        // Phase 1: cardinality of the candidate cluster for every seed\n        const int grid = std::min(num_seeds, grid_max);\n        growClustersKernel<<<grid, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n            d_points, d_distmat, d_clustered, d_seeds, num_seeds, N, * num_seeds, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        // Pick the best seed: highest cardinality, first in seed order on ties\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        for d_points, d_distmat, d_clustered, d_seeds, 1, N, threshold,\n                d_workspace, d_card, d_members);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_members_host.data(), d_members,\n                                  sizeof(int) * max_cardinality,\n                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members.assign(best_members_host.begin(),\n                                   best_members_host.begin() + clustered[cluster.members[i]] = 1;\n            }\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    cudaFree(d_points);\n    cudaFree(d_clustered);\n    cudaFree(d_seeds);\n clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n\n    // Initialize the CUDA context up front so one-time device setup\n    // is not attributed to the clustering time\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0));","originalFile":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - CUDA Parallel Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//  - Every unclustered point is tried as a seed; each candidate cluster is\n//    grown by one CUDA thread block (grid-stride over seeds).\n//  - Each block keeps, for every point, the maximum distance to the current\n//    cluster members (\"min-diameter if added\"), updated incrementally after\n//    each addition, and selects the next member with a block-wide argmin\n//    reduction that breaks ties toward the lowest point index, exactly\n//    matching the sequential candidate scan order.\n//  - When it fits in GPU memory, the full pairwise distance matrix is\n//    precomputed once and reused across all iterations.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                           \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,  \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__));                               \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\nstatic const int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\nstatic const int MAX_GRID_BLOCKS = 512;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (host)\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (device)\n__device__ inline double pointDist(const double2 a, const double2 b) {\n    const double dx = a.x - b.x;\n    const double dy = a.y - b.y;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Precompute the full pairwise distance matrix\n__global__ void distMatrixKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                                 double* __restrict__ distmat,\n                                 const int N) {\n    const int j = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = blockIdx.y;\n    if (j < N) {\n        distmat[static_cast<size_t>(i) * N + j] = pointDist(points[i], points[j]);\n    }\n}\n\n// Grow a candidate cluster for each seed (one thread block per seed,\n// grid-stride over seeds) and record its cardinality. For every point j,\n// md[j] holds the maximum distance from j to the current cluster members,\n// i.e. the cluster diameter if j were added; md[j] = -1 marks members of\n// this candidate cluster and md[j] = DBL_MAX marks globally clustered\n// points (both are never selected). The next member is the eligible point\n// (md < threshold) with the smallest md, ties broken toward the lowest\n// index to match the sequential scan.\n// If members_out is non-null (single-seed rebuild launch), the members are\n// recorded in selection order.\n__global__ void growClustersKernel(const double2* __restrict__ points,\n                                   const double* __restrict__ distmat,\n                                   const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                   const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                                   const int num_seeds,\n                                   const int N,\n                                   const double threshold,\n                                   double* __restrict__ workspace,\n                                   int* __restrict__ cardinalities,\n                                   int* __restrict__ members_out) {\n    __shared__ double sval[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int sidx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ int chosen;\n\n    double* md = workspace + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * N;\n\n    for (int s = blockIdx.x; s < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[s];\n        const double2 seed_pt = points[seed];\n        const double* seed_row =\n            distmat ? distmat + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * N : nullptr;\n\n        for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < N; j += blockDim.x) {\n            if (j == seed) {\n                md[j] = -1.0;\n            } else if (clustered[j]) {\n                md[j] = DBL_MAX;\n            } else {\n                md[j] = seed_row ? seed_row[j] : pointDist(seed_pt, points[j]);\n            }\n        }\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0 && members_out) {\n            members_out[0] = seed;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        int count = 1;\n        while (count < N) {\n            // Block-wide argmin over eligible candidates\n            double best_val = DBL_MAX;\n            int best_idx = -1;\n            for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < N; j += blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = md[j];\n                if (v >= 0.0 && v < threshold && v < best_val) {\n                    best_val = v;\n                    best_idx = j;\n                }\n            }\n            sval[threadIdx.x] = best_val;\n            sidx[threadIdx.x] = best_idx;\n            __syncthreads();\n            for (int off = blockDim.x / 2; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n                if (threadIdx.x < off) {\n                    const double ov = sval[threadIdx.x + off];\n                    const int oi = sidx[threadIdx.x + off];\n                    if (oi >= 0 &&\n                        (sidx[threadIdx.x] < 0 || ov < sval[threadIdx.x] ||\n                         (ov == sval[threadIdx.x] && oi < sidx[threadIdx.x]))) {\n                        sval[threadIdx.x] = ov;\n                        sidx[threadIdx.x] = oi;\n                    }\n                }\n                __syncthreads();\n            }\n            if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n                chosen = sidx[0];\n                if (chosen >= 0) {\n                    md[chosen] = -1.0;\n                    if (members_out) {\n                        members_out[count] = chosen;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n            const int c = chosen;\n            if (c < 0) break;  // No more points can be added\n            count++;\n\n            // Fold the new member's distances into md\n            const double2 c_pt = points[c];\n            const double* c_row =\n                distmat ? distmat + static_cast<size_t>(c) * N : nullptr;\n            for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < N; j += blockDim.x) {\n                const double v = md[j];\n                if (v >= 0.0 && v < DBL_MAX) {\n                    const double d = c_row ? c_row[j] : pointDist(c_pt, points[j]);\n                    if (d > v) {\n                        md[j] = d;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n            cardinalities[s] = count;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (CUDA)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    // Device buffers\n    double2* d_points = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_seeds = nullptr;\n    int* d_card = nullptr;\n    int* d_members = nullptr;\n    double* d_workspace = nullptr;\n    double* d_distmat = nullptr;\n\n    const int grid_max = std::min(N, MAX_GRID_BLOCKS);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, sizeof(double2) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, sizeof(unsigned char) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_card, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_workspace,\n                          sizeof(double) * static_cast<size_t>(grid_max) * N));\n\n    static_assert(sizeof(Point) == sizeof(double2), \"Point/double2 layout mismatch\");\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), sizeof(double2) * N,\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // Precompute the pairwise distance matrix when it fits comfortably\n    {\n        size_t free_mem = 0, total_mem = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_mem, &total_mem));\n        const size_t distmat_bytes = sizeof(double) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N;\n        if (distmat_bytes < static_cast<size_t>(0.8 * free_mem)) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_distmat, distmat_bytes));\n            const dim3 dm_grid((N + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE, N);\n            distMatrixKernel<<<dm_grid, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_points, d_distmat, N);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cardinalities(N);\n    std::vector<int> best_members_host(N);\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int num_seeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, clustered.data(),\n                              sizeof(unsigned char) * N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds, unclustered_indices.data(),\n                              sizeof(int) * num_seeds, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n        // Phase 1: cardinality of the candidate cluster for every seed\n        const int grid = std::min(num_seeds, grid_max);\n        growClustersKernel<<<grid, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n            d_points, d_distmat, d_clustered, d_seeds, num_seeds, N, threshold,\n            d_workspace, d_card, nullptr);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(cardinalities.data(), d_card,\n                              sizeof(int) * num_seeds, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        // Pick the best seed: highest cardinality, first in seed order on ties\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        for (int i = 0; i < num_seeds; ++i) {\n            if (cardinalities[i] > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinalities[i];\n                best_seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            // Phase 2: rebuild the winning cluster, recording its members\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds, &best_seed, sizeof(int),\n                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            growClustersKernel<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n                d_points, d_distmat, d_clustered, d_seeds, 1, N, threshold,\n                d_workspace, d_card, d_members);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(best_members_host.data(), d_members,\n                                  sizeof(int) * max_cardinality,\n                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members.assign(best_members_host.begin(),\n                                   best_members_host.begin() + max_cardinality);\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n\n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[cluster.members[i]] = 1;\n            }\n\n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    cudaFree(d_points);\n    cudaFree(d_clustered);\n    cudaFree(d_seeds);\n    cudaFree(d_card);\n    cudaFree(d_members);\n    cudaFree(d_workspace);\n    if (d_distmat) cudaFree(d_distmat);\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n\n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    const std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n\n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n\n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n\n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :\n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n\n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\",\n           total_clustered, // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\",\n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n The QT clustering benchmark is now CUDA-parallel with bitwise-identical results to the sequential original.\n\n## What was done\n\n**`qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp`** — the whole file now compiles as CUDA. The core `qtClustering` loop keeps the original semantics (repeatedly grow a candidate cluster from every unclustered seed, keep the largest, repeat), but the expensive part runs on the GPU:\n\n- **One thread block per seed** (grid-striding over up to 512 blocks) grows that seed's candidate cluster. Instead of rescanning all members per candidate (the original's O(members) inner loop), each block maintains an array of \"max distance to current cluster members\" per point, updated incrementally after every addition — same values, since max is order-independent and exact.\n- The next member is picked with a **block-wide argmin reduction that breaks ties toward the lowest point index**, exactly matching the sequential scan order, so cluster construction is deterministic and identical.\n- When it fits in 80% of free GPU memory, the **full N×N pairwise distance matrix is precomputed once** and reused everywhere; otherwise distances are computed on the fly (both paths verified to give identical hashes).\n- Phase 1 computes only cardinalities for all seeds; the winning cluster is then rebuilt once with member recording.
